## Words 3,001 – 3,100

### 3001. [abolish](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/abolish) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈbɒlɪʃ/` · **US:** `/əˈbɑːlɪʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/abo/aboli/abolish__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/abo/aboli/abolish__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हटाना, मिटा देना, उन्मूलन करना
- **Examples:**
  - This tax should be abolished.

### 3002. [abrupt](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/abrupt)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/abrupt)
- **Meaning:** उखड़ा उखड़ा, आकस्मिक, असंगत

- **UK:** `/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/` · **US:** `/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/accum/accumulate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/accum/accumulate__us_1.mp3)

### 3010. [acute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/acute) *(adjective)*

### 3011. [adhere](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/adhere) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ədˈhɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ədˈhɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adh/adher/adhere__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adh/adher/adhere__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पालन करना, समर्थन करना, पालन
- **Examples:**

  - Clean the surface first, or the paint will not adhere.
  - There was oil adhering to the bird’s feathers.
  - The tiles may not adhere properly if you do not use the correct glue.

### 3023. [allege](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/allege) *(verb)*

- **Examples:**

  - it is alleged (that)… It is alleged that he mistreated the prisoners.

  - allege something This procedure should be followed in cases where dishonesty has been alleged.

### 3024. [allocate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/allocate) *(verb)*

- **Examples:**

  - allocate something (to somebody/something) They intend to allocate more places to mature students this year.

  - allocate somebody/something sth The project is being allocated more resources.

- **Meaning:** अभिलाषा/चाह, महत्वाकांक्षा, महाप्राण  ध्वनि उच्चारण
- **Examples:**
  - I didn't realize you had political aspirations.
  - aspiration to do something He has never had any aspiration to earn a lot of money.
  - aspiration for something What changes are needed to meet women's aspirations for employment?

### 3048. [assert](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/assert) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈsɜːt/` · **US:** `/əˈsɜːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/asser/assert__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/asser/assert__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दिखाना, जोर देना, दृढतापूर्वक कहना
- **Examples:**
  - assert that… She continued to assert that she was innocent.
  - The report asserts confidently that the industry will grow.
  - assert something She continued to assert her innocence.
  - The German Chancellor was anxious to assert her government's commitment to European unity.

- **Meaning:** शुद्ध करना, स्पष्टीकरण देना, स्पष्ट करना
- **Examples:**
  - clarify something to clarify a situation/problem/issue
  - I hope this clarifies my position.
  - The law on drugs needs to be clarified.
  - Talking to someone has helped clarify my feelings.

### 3497. [inspiration](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/inspiration) *(noun)*

### 3498. [inspire](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/inspire) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈspaɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈspaɪər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/inspi/inspire__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/inspi/inspire__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उत्पन्न होना, प्रेरित करना, भर देना
- **Examples:**
  - inspire somebody The actors' enthusiasm inspired the kids.
  - inspire somebody with something The actors inspired the kids with their enthusiasm.
  - inspire somebody to something His superb play inspired the team to a thrilling 5–0 win.
  - inspire somebody to do something By visiting schools, the actors hope to inspire children to put on their own productions.

### 3702. [notify](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/notify) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnəʊtɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/ˈnəʊtɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/notif/notify__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/notif/notify__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सूचित करना, सूचना देना
- **Examples:**
  - notify somebody Competition winners will be notified by post.
  - You must notify us in writing if you wish to cancel your subscription.
  - notify somebody of something The police must be notified of the date of the demonstration.
  - notify something to somebody The date of the demonstration must be notified to the police.

- **Meaning:** कुख्यात, बदनाम
- **Examples:**

- **Meaning:** प्रभाव, प्रगति मे बाधा, प्रतिक्रिया
- **Examples:**
  - to provoke/cause/get a reaction
  - a positive/negative/adverse reaction
  - reaction to something What was his reaction to the news?
  - Often our actions are based upon an emotional reaction to what someone has done or said to us.

### 3974. [reassure](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reassure) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌriːəˈʃɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/ˌriːəˈʃʊr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/reass/reassure__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/reass/reassure__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

### 3981. [recall](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/recall_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈkɔːl/` · **US:** `/rɪˈkɔːl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rec/recal/recall__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rec/recal/recall__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वापस लेना, वापस बुलाना, याद करना
- **Examples:**
  - recall something She could not recall his name.
  - Many years later Muir recalled his days at Glasgow University.
  - I cannot recall a time when the country faced such serious problems.
  - ‘I may have; I don't recall,’ she said.

### 3990. [recognition](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/recognition) *(noun)*

### 3991. [recognize](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/recognize) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈrekəɡnaɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈrekəɡnaɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rec/recog/recognize__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rec/recog/recognize__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वीकार करना, प्रकट करना, पहचानना
- **Examples:**
  - recognize somebody/something I recognized him as soon as he came in the room.
  - Do you recognize this tune?
  - You might not recognize the name but you'll know her face.
  - I recognized the voice immediately.
